│ re.IGNORECASE | re.DOTALL)\ntext = style_re.sub(sanitize_style_block,        │
│ text)\n\n# 3) Remove event handler attributes like onclick, onerror, onload, │
│ etc.\n# We remove the whole attribute (including preceding                   │
│ whitespace).\nevent_attr_re =                                                │
│ re.compile(r"\s+on[a-zA-Z0-9:-]+\s*=\s*(?:\"[^\"]\"|&#x27;[^&#x27;]&#x27;|[^ │
│ \s>]+)", re.IGNORECASE)\ntext = event_attr_re.sub(&#x27;&#x27;, text)\n\n#   │
│ 4) Sanitize attribute values: remove javascript:, vbscript:, and dangerous   │
│ data: MIME types\n# We will process quoted attributes first, then            │
│ unquoted.\nattr_quoted_re =                                                  │
│ re.compile(r"(\s+[\w:-]+\s*=\s*)([\&#x27;\"])(.?)\2", re.DOTALL)\n\ndef      │
│ _sanitize_quoted_attr(m):\n    prefix = m.group(1)\n    quote = m.group(2)\n │
│ val = m.group(3)\n    low = val.lstrip().lower()\n    # remove javascript:   │
│ and vbscript: at start\n    if low.startswith(&#x27;javascript:&#x27;) or    │
│ low.startswith(&#x27;vbscript:&#x27;):\n        return prefix + quote +      │
│ quote\n    # remove dangerous data: MIME types\n    if                       │
│ low.startswith(&#x27;data:&#x27;):\n        # capture mime type\n        mm  │
│ = re.match(r&#x27;data:([^;,]+)&#x27;, low)\n        if mm:\n                │
│ mime = mm.group(1)\n            if any(s in mime for s in                    │
│ (&#x27;text/html&#x27;, &#x27;text/javascript&#x27;,                         │
│ &#x27;application/javascript&#x27;, &#x27;application/x-javascript&#x27;,    │
│ &#x27;image/svg+xml&#x27;)):\n                return prefix + quote +        │
│ quote\n    # sanitize style-like payloads inside style attributes\n    if    │
│ prefix.strip().lower().startswith(&#x27;style=&#x27;):\n        v2 =         │
│ re.sub(r"expression\s\([^)]\)", "", val, flags=re.IGNORECASE)\n        v2 =  │
│ re.sub(r"javascript\s:\s*", "", v2, flags=re.IGNORECASE)\n        return     │
│ prefix + quote + v2 + quote\n    # otherwise remove any javascript:          │
│ occurrences inside the value\n    val2 = re.sub(r"javascript\s*:\s*", "",    │
│ val, flags=re.IGNORECASE)\n    # also remove vbscript:\n    val2 =           │
│ re.sub(r"vbscript\s*:\s*", "", val2, flags=re.IGNORECASE)\n    return prefix │
│ + quote + val2 + quote\n\ntext = attr_quoted_re.sub(_sanitize_quoted_attr,   │
│ text)\n\n# Unquoted attributes: name=value (without                          │
│ quotes)\nattr_unquoted_re =                                                  │
│ re.compile(r"(\s+[\w:-]+\s*=\s*)([^\s>]+)")\n\ndef                           │
│ _sanitize_unquoted_attr(m):\n    prefix = m.group(1)\n    val = m.group(2)\n │
│ low = val.lstrip().lower()\n    if low.startswith(&#x27;javascript:&#x27;)   │
│ or low.startswith(&#x27;vbscript:&#x27;):\n        return prefix +           │
│ &#x27;&#x27;\n    if low.startswith(&#x27;data:&#x27;):\n        mm =        │
│ re.match(r&#x27;data:([^;,]+)&#x27;, low)\n        if mm:\n            mime  │
│ = mm.group(1)\n            if any(s in mime for s in (&#x27;text/html&#x27;, │
│ &#x27;text/javascript&#x27;, &#x27;application/javascript&#x27;,             │
│ &#x27;application/x-javascript&#x27;, &#x27;image/svg+xml&#x27;)):\n         │
│ return prefix + &#x27;&#x27;\n    # remove javascript: or vbscript: inside   │
│ the unquoted value\n    val2 = re.sub(r"javascript\s*:\s*", "", val,         │
│ flags=re.IGNORECASE)\n    val2 = re.sub(r"vbscript\s*:\s*", "", val2,        │
│ flags=re.IGNORECASE)\n    return prefix + val2\n\ntext =                     │
│ attr_unquoted_re.sub(_sanitize_unquoted_attr, text)\n\n# 5) Remove meta      │
│ refresh javascript URLs inside content attribute if any (extra               │
│ guard)\nmeta_re =                                                            │
│ re.compile(r"(<meta\b[^>]http-equiv\s=\s*[&#x27;\"]?refresh[&#x27;\"]?[^>]>) │
│ ", re.IGNORECASE)\n\ndef _sanitize_meta(m):\n    tag = m.group(1)\n    #     │
│ sanitize any content="..." inside tag\n    def repl_content(cm):\n           │
│ pre = cm.group(1)\n        quote = cm.group(2)\n        val = cm.group(3)\n  │
│ # remove javascript: occurrences\n        val2 = re.sub(r"javascript\s:\s*", │
│ "", val, flags=re.IGNORECASE)\n        return pre + quote + val2 + quote\n   │
│ tag2 = re.sub(r"(content\s*=\s*)([\&#x27;\"])(.?)\2", repl_content, tag,     │
│ flags=re.IGNORECASE | re.DOTALL)\n    return tag2\n\ntext =                  │
│ meta_re.sub(_sanitize_meta, text)\n\n# Final pass: remove any remaining      │
│ standalone javascript: or vbscript: schemes (rare)\ntext =                   │
│ re.sub(r"javascript\s:\s*", "", text, flags=re.IGNORECASE)\ntext =           │
│ re.sub(r"vbscript\s*:\s*", "", text, flags=re.IGNORECASE)\n\n# Write         │
│ atomically\ntmp = path + &#x27;.tmp&#x27;\nwith open(tmp, &#x27;wb&#x27;) as │
│ f:\n    f.write(text.encode(encoding))\n# replace original\nos.replace(tmp,  │
│ path)\n\nif name == &#x27;main&#x27;:\n    pass\nPY'}                        │

│ ┏━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┓ │
│ ┃         We will process quoted attributes first, then unquoted.          ┃ │
│ ┗━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┛ │

│ attr_quoted_re = re.compile(r"(\s+[\w:-]+\s*=\s*)([&#x27;&quot;])(.*?)\2",   │
│ re.DOTALL)                                                                   │
│                                                                              │
│ def _sanitize_quoted_attr(m): prefix = m.group(1) quote = m.group(2) val =   │
│ m.group(3) low = val.lstrip().lower() # remove javascript: and vbscript: at  │
│ start if low.startswith('javascript:') or low.startswith('vbscript:'):       │
│ return prefix + quote + quote # remove dangerous data: MIME types if         │
│ low.startswith('data:'): # capture mime type mm = re.match(r'data:([^;,]+)', │
│ low) if mm: mime = mm.group(1) if any(s in mime for s in ('text/html',       │
│ 'text/javascript', 'application/javascript', 'application/x-javascript',     │
│ 'image/svg+xml')): return prefix + quote + quote # sanitize style-like       │
│ payloads inside style attributes if                                          │
│ prefix.strip().lower().startswith('style='): v2 =                            │
│ re.sub(r"expression\s*([^)])", "", val, flags=re.IGNORECASE) v2 =            │
│ re.sub(r"javascript\s:\s*", "", v2, flags=re.IGNORECASE) return prefix +     │
│ quote + v2 + quote # otherwise remove any javascript: occurrences inside the │
│ value val2 = re.sub(r"javascript\s*:\s*", "", val, flags=re.IGNORECASE) #    │
│ also remove vbscript: val2 = re.sub(r"vbscript\s*:\s*", "", val2,            │
│ flags=re.IGNORECASE) return prefix + quote + val2 + quote                    │
│                                                                              │
│ text = attr_quoted_re.sub(_sanitize_quoted_attr, text)                       │
│                                                                              │
│ ┏━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┓ │
│ ┃             Unquoted attributes: name=value (without quotes)             ┃ │
│ ┗━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┛ │
│                                                                              │
│ attr_unquoted_re = re.compile(r"(\s+[\w:-]+\s*=\s*)([^\s>]+)")               │
│                                                                              │
│ def _sanitize_unquoted_attr(m): prefix = m.group(1) val = m.group(2) low =   │
│ val.lstrip().lower() if low.startswith('javascript:') or                     │
│ low.startswith('vbscript:'): return prefix + '' if low.startswith('data:'):  │
│ mm = re.match(r'data:([^;,]+)', low) if mm: mime = mm.group(1) if any(s in   │
│ mime for s in ('text/html', 'text/javascript', 'application/javascript',     │
│ 'application/x-javascript', 'image/svg+xml')): return prefix + '' # remove   │
│ javascript: or vbscript: inside the unquoted value val2 =                    │
│ re.sub(r"javascript\s*:\s*", "", val, flags=re.IGNORECASE) val2 =            │
│ re.sub(r"vbscript\s*:\s*", "", val2, flags=re.IGNORECASE) return prefix +    │
│ val2                                                                         │
│                                                                              │
│ text = attr_unquoted_re.sub(_sanitize_unquoted_attr, text)                   │
│                                                                              │
│ ┏━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┓ │
│ ┃  5) Remove meta refresh javascript URLs inside content attribute if any  ┃ │
│ ┃                              (extra guard)                               ┃ │
│ ┗━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┛ │

│ meta_re =                                                                    │
│ re.compile(r"(<meta\b[^>]http-equiv\s=\s*['&quot;]?refresh['&quot;]?[^>]*>)" │
│ , re.IGNORECASE)                                                             │
│                                                                              │
│ def _sanitize_meta(m): tag = m.group(1) # sanitize any content="..." inside  │
│ tag def repl_content(cm): pre = cm.group(1) quote = cm.group(2) val =        │
│ cm.group(3) # remove javascript: occurrences val2 =                          │
│ re.sub(r"javascript\s*:\s*", "", val, flags=re.IGNORECASE) return pre +      │
│ quote + val2 + quote tag2 =                                                  │
│ re.sub(r"(content\s*=\s*)([&#x27;&quot;])(.*?)\2", repl_content, tag,        │
│ flags=re.IGNORECASE | re.DOTALL) return tag2                                 │
│                                                                              │
│ text = meta_re.sub(_sanitize_meta, text)                                     │
│                                                                              │
│ ┏━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┓ │
│ ┃   Final pass: remove any remaining standalone javascript: or vbscript:   ┃ │

│ finish_reason='stop' content='I created /app/filter.py and tested it.        │
│ Summary of what I did, how it works, and limitations below.\n\nWhat I        │
│ created\n- File: /app/filter.py (executable)\n- Purpose: remove              │
│ JavaScript/injectable script content/attributes from an HTML file passed as  │
│ argv[1], modifying the file in-place (atomic replace).\n\nHow it works (high │
│ level)\n- Reads the file as raw bytes and decodes preserving                 │
│ encoding/newlines (falls back to latin-1 if utf-8 decoding fails).\n-        │
│ Performs minimally-invasive, regex-based edits on the raw text to avoid full │
│ DOM reserialization (to better preserve formatting).\n- Main                 │
│ removals/sanitizations:\n  - Remove <script>...</script> blocks (and         │
│ self-closing <script/>).\n  - Remove event-handler attributes (attributes    │
│ starting with "on", e.g., onclick, onload, onerror) — removes the whole      │
│ attribute.\n  - Sanitize style blocks: remove CSS expression(...) and remove │
│ javascript: occurrences inside style content.\n  - Sanitize quoted and       │
│ unquoted attribute values:\n    - Remove javascript: and vbscript:           │
│ schemes.\n    - Strip data: URIs with dangerous mimetypes (text/html,        │
│ text/javascript, application/javascript, application/x-javascript,           │
│ image/svg+xml) by blanking the attribute.\n    - For style attributes,       │
│ additionally remove expression and javascript: snippets.\n  - Sanitize <meta │
│ http-equiv="refresh"> content attribute (removes javascript: occurrences     │
│ inside content value).\n  - Final pass: remove any remaining javascript: /   │
│ vbscript: substrings.\n- Writes out the updated content to a temporary file  │
│ and atomically replaces the original.\n\nUsage\n- Run: python3               │
│ /app/filter.py /path/to/file.html\n- The file is modified in-place. Ensure   │
│ you have backups if needed.\n\nExample test performed\n- I created           │
│ /app/test_input.html with several javascript vectors (inline <script>,       │
│ javascript: href, data: src with HTML, event handlers, CSS expression).\n-   │
│ After running the script the file was modified: script elements removed,     │
│ event attributes removed, javascript:/vbscript: schemes removed, and data:   │
│ attributes with dangerous MIME types blanked.\n\nLimitations and notes\n-    │
│ Preserving "exact formatting in every byte" is extremely difficult when      │
│ sanitizing; this script uses raw-text regex edits to minimize reformatting,  │
│ but some changes are necessary to remove the harmful substrings (e.g.,       │
│ attributes removed or blanked, script tag removals). The script aims to      │
│ preserve surrounding whitespace/newlines and other HTML structure as much as │
│ reasonably possible.\n- Regex-based sanitization cannot cover every          │
│ obfuscated/edge-case JavaScript injection; this implementation handles       │
│ common vectors: <script> tags, event handler attributes,                     │
│ javascript:/vbscript: schemes, dangerous data: MIME types, CSS expression(), │
│ meta refresh with javascript. For full protection in production, a robust    │
│ HTML sanitizer library or an allowlist approach is recommended.\n- The       │
│ script blanks attribute values if they contain dangerous schemes or data     │
│ MIME types rather than trying to reconstruct safe equivalents — this is      │
│ safer than leaving altered-but-still-dangerous content.\n- It performs an    │
│ atomic replace (write to .tmp and os.replace) to reduce risk of corrupting   │
│ the file.\n\nWhere to find it\n- /app/filter.py (executable after            │
│ creation)\n\nActions performed (commands run)\n- Created /app/filter.py,     │

│ attributes/tags.\n\n[tester]' usage=RequestUsage(prompt_tokens=10228,        │

│                                                                              │
│ What I created                                                               │
│                                                                              │
│  • File: /app/filter.py (executable)                                         │
│  • Purpose: remove JavaScript/injectable script content/attributes from an   │
│    HTML file passed as argv[1], modifying the file in-place (atomic          │
│    replace).                                                                 │
│                                                                              │
│ How it works (high level)                                                    │
│                                                                              │
│  • Reads the file as raw bytes and decodes preserving encoding/newlines      │
│    (falls back to latin-1 if utf-8 decoding fails).                          │
│  • Performs minimally-invasive, regex-based edits on the raw text to avoid   │
│    full DOM reserialization (to better preserve formatting).                 │
│  • Main removals/sanitizations:                                              │
│     • Remove <script>...</script> blocks (and self-closing <script/>).       │
│     • Remove event-handler attributes (attributes starting with "on", e.g.,  │
│       onclick, onload, onerror) — removes the whole attribute.               │
│     • Sanitize style blocks: remove CSS expression(...) and remove           │
│       javascript: occurrences inside style content.                          │
│     • Sanitize quoted and unquoted attribute values:                         │
│        • Remove javascript: and vbscript: schemes.                           │
│        • Strip data: URIs with dangerous mimetypes (text/html,               │
│          text/javascript, application/javascript, application/x-javascript,  │
│          image/svg+xml) by blanking the attribute.                           │
│        • For style attributes, additionally remove expression and            │
│          javascript: snippets.                                               │
│     • Sanitize <meta http-equiv="refresh"> content attribute (removes        │
│       javascript: occurrences inside content value).                         │
│     • Final pass: remove any remaining javascript: / vbscript: substrings.   │
│  • Writes out the updated content to a temporary file and atomically         │
│    replaces the original.                                                    │
│                                                                              │
│ Usage                                                                        │
│                                                                              │
│  • Run: python3 /app/filter.py /path/to/file.html                            │
│  • The file is modified in-place. Ensure you have backups if needed.         │
│                                                                              │
│ Example test performed                                                       │
│                                                                              │
│  • I created /app/test_input.html with several javascript vectors (inline    │
│    <script>, javascript: href, data: src with HTML, event handlers, CSS      │
│    expression).                                                              │
│  • After running the script the file was modified: script elements removed,  │
│    event attributes removed, javascript:/vbscript: schemes removed, and      │
│    data: attributes with dangerous MIME types blanked.                       │
│                                                                              │
│ Limitations and notes                                                        │
│                                                                              │
│  • Preserving "exact formatting in every byte" is extremely difficult when   │
│    sanitizing; this script uses raw-text regex edits to minimize             │
│    reformatting, but some changes are necessary to remove the harmful        │
│    substrings (e.g., attributes removed or blanked, script tag removals).    │
│    The script aims to preserve surrounding whitespace/newlines and other     │
│    HTML structure as much as reasonably possible.                            │
│  • Regex-based sanitization cannot cover every obfuscated/edge-case          │
│    JavaScript injection; this implementation handles common vectors:         │
│    <script> tags, event handler attributes, javascript:/vbscript: schemes,   │
│    dangerous data: MIME types, CSS expression(), meta refresh with           │
│    javascript. For full protection in production, a robust HTML sanitizer    │
│    library or an allowlist approach is recommended.                          │
│  • The script blanks attribute values if they contain dangerous schemes or   │
│    data MIME types rather than trying to reconstruct safe equivalents — this │
│    is safer than leaving altered-but-still-dangerous content.                │
│  • It performs an atomic replace (write to .tmp and os.replace) to reduce    │
│    risk of corrupting the file.                                              │
│                                                                              │
│ Where to find it                                                             │
│                                                                              │
│  • /app/filter.py (executable after creation)                                │
│                                                                              │
│ Actions performed (commands run)                                             │
│                                                                              │
│  • Created /app/filter.py, made it executable                                │
│  • Created a test HTML and ran the filter against it to verify effect        │
│  • Verified the output                                                       │
│                                                                              │
│ If you want, I can:                                                          │
│                                                                              │
│  • Harden or extend the filter to cover additional vectors (SVG script,      │
│    nested data URIs, CSS url() patterns more strictly).                      │
│  • Add a dry-run mode that writes to stdout instead of replacing the file.   │
│  • Add logging or an allowlist of safe attributes/tags.                      │
│                                                                              │

│ b.decode(encoding)                                                           │
│                                                                              │
│ ┏━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┓ │
│ ┃        Remove <script>...</script> blocks (including attributes)         ┃ │
│ ┗━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┛ │

│ text = re.sub(r"<script\b[^>]?>.?</script\s*>", '', text,                    │

│ ┏━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┓ │
│ ┃                     Remove self-closing script tags                      ┃ │
│ ┗━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┛ │
│                                                                              │
│ text = re.sub(r"<script\b[^>]*/>", '', text, flags=re.IGNORECASE |           │
│ re.DOTALL)                                                                   │
│                                                                              │
│ ┏━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┓ │
│ ┃   Sanitize <style> blocks: remove CSS expression(...) and javascript:    ┃ │
│ ┃                               occurrences                                ┃ │
│ ┗━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┛ │
│                                                                              │
│ def _sanitize_style(m): open_tag = m.group(1) body = m.group(2) close_tag =  │
│ m.group(3) body2 = re.sub(r"expression\s*([^)])", '', body,                  │
│ flags=re.IGNORECASE) body2 = re.sub(r"javascript\s:\s*", '', body2,          │
│ flags=re.IGNORECASE) return open_tag + body2 + close_tag                     │
│                                                                              │
│ text = re.sub(r"(<style\b[^>]>)(.?)(</style>)", _sanitize_style, text,       │
│ flags=re.IGNORECASE | re.DOTALL)                                             │
│                                                                              │
│ ┏━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┓ │
│ ┃     Remove event handler attributes (onclick, onerror, onload, etc.)     ┃ │
│ ┗━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┛ │
│                                                                              │
│ text =                                                                       │
│ re.sub(r"\s+on[\w:-]+\s*=\s*(?:&quot;[^&quot;]&quot;|'[^']'|[^\s>]+)", '',   │
│ text, flags=re.IGNORECASE)                                                   │
│                                                                              │
│ ┏━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┓ │
│ ┃ Sanitize quoted attributes: remove javascript:, vbscript:, and dangerous ┃ │
│ ┃                             data: mimetypes                              ┃ │
│ ┗━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┛ │
│                                                                              │
│ attr_quoted_re = re.compile(r"(\s+[\w:-]+\s*=\s*)([&#x27;&quot;])(.*?)\2",   │
│ re.DOTALL)                                                                   │
│                                                                              │
│ def _sanitize_quoted(m): prefix = m.group(1) quote = m.group(2) val =        │
│ m.group(3) low = val.lstrip().lower() if low.startswith('javascript:') or    │
│ low.startswith('vbscript:'): return prefix + quote + quote if                │
│ low.startswith('data:'): mm = re.match(r'data:([^;,]+)', low) if mm: mime =  │
│ mm.group(1) if any(t in mime for t in ('text/html', 'text/javascript',       │
│ 'application/javascript', 'application/x-javascript', 'image/svg+xml')):     │
│ return prefix + quote + quote # style attribute: remove expressions and      │
│ schemes if prefix.strip().lower().startswith('style='): v2 =                 │
│ re.sub(r"expression\s*([^)])", '', val, flags=re.IGNORECASE) v2 =            │
│ re.sub(r"javascript\s:\s*", '', v2, flags=re.IGNORECASE) return prefix +     │
│ quote + v2 + quote # otherwise strip javascript/vbscript schemes inside      │
│ value v2 = re.sub(r"javascript\s*:\s*", '', val, flags=re.IGNORECASE) v2 =   │
│ re.sub(r"vbscript\s*:\s*", '', v2, flags=re.IGNORECASE) return prefix +      │
│ quote + v2 + quote                                                           │
│                                                                              │
│ text = attr_quoted_re.sub(_sanitize_quoted, text)                            │
│                                                                              │
│ ┏━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┓ │
│ ┃                       Sanitize unquoted attributes                       ┃ │
│ ┗━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┛ │
│                                                                              │
│ attr_unquoted_re = re.compile(r"(\s+[\w:-]+\s*=\s*)([^\s>]+)")               │
│                                                                              │
│ def _sanitize_unquoted(m): prefix = m.group(1) val = m.group(2) low =        │
│ val.lstrip().lower() if low.startswith('javascript:') or                     │
│ low.startswith('vbscript:'): return prefix if low.startswith('data:'): mm =  │
│ re.match(r'data:([^;,]+)', low) if mm: mime = mm.group(1) if any(t in mime   │
│ for t in ('text/html', 'text/javascript', 'application/javascript',          │
│ 'application/x-javascript', 'image/svg+xml')): return prefix v2 =            │
│ re.sub(r"javascript\s*:\s*", '', val, flags=re.IGNORECASE) v2 =              │
│ re.sub(r"vbscript\s*:\s*", '', v2, flags=re.IGNORECASE) return prefix + v2   │
│                                                                              │
│ text = attr_unquoted_re.sub(_sanitize_unquoted, text)                        │
│                                                                              │
│ ┏━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┓ │
│ ┃                 Sanitize meta refresh content attributes                 ┃ │
│ ┗━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┛ │
│                                                                              │
│ def _sanitize_meta(m): tag = m.group(1) def repl(cm): pre = cm.group(1)      │
│ quote = cm.group(2) val = cm.group(3) v2 = re.sub(r"javascript\s*:\s*", '',  │
│ val, flags=re.IGNORECASE) return pre + quote + v2 + quote tag2 =             │
│ re.sub(r"(content\s*=\s*)([&#x27;&quot;])(.*?)\2", repl, tag,                │
│ flags=re.IGNORECASE | re.DOTALL) return tag2                                 │
│                                                                              │
│ text =                                                                       │
│ re.sub(r"(<meta\b[^>]http-equiv\s=\s*['&quot;]?refresh['&quot;]?[^>]*>)",    │
│ _sanitize_meta, text, flags=re.IGNORECASE)                                   │
│                                                                              │
│ ┏━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┓ │
│ ┃     Final cleanup: remove leftover javascript: and vbscript: schemes     ┃ │
│ ┗━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┛ │
│                                                                              │
│ text = re.sub(r"javascript\s*:\s*", '', text, flags=re.IGNORECASE) text =    │
│ re.sub(r"vbscript\s*:\s*", '', text, flags=re.IGNORECASE)                    │
│                                                                              │
│ ┏━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┓ │
│ ┃                             Write atomically                             ┃ │
│ ┗━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┛ │
│                                                                              │
│ tmp = path + '.tmp' with open(tmp, 'wb') as f:                               │
│ f.write(text.encode(encoding)) os.replace(tmp, path)                         │
│                                                                              │
│ if name == 'main': pass </output>                                            │

│ ->  <div style="background-image: url(&#x27;alert(1)&#x27;); ...">\n  -      │

│ content="0;url=javascript:alert(&#x27;xss&#x27;)">  ->  <meta                │
│ http-equiv="refresh" content="0;url=alert(&#x27;xss&#x27;)">\n\nWhy this is  │
│ not fully correct for the task [solver]\n- The filter performs a global      │
│ removal of the substring javascript: (and vbscript:) across the whole        │
│ document (final cleanup step). That caused modifications to non-executable   │
│ text such as comments and other textual content. The task demanded "Do not   │
│ alter the formatting of the HTML content in any way" and "functionally       │
│ identical to the input except for the removal of harmful substrings" —       │
│ removing the literal "javascript:" inside comments or unrelated text is an   │
│ alteration of non-harmful content.\n- Some sanitizations are overly          │
│ aggressive or incomplete:\n  - style url(&#x27;javascript:alert(1)&#x27;)    │
│ became url(&#x27;alert(1)&#x27;) — the scheme was removed but the remaining  │
│ payload (alert(1)) remains in the document. This may leave                   │
│ visible/functional artifacts and did not fully neutralize the vector (the    │
│ url(...) should be removed or replaced safely).\n  - meta refresh was        │
│ changed to url=alert(&#x27;xss&#x27;) (scheme removed) leaving non-URL text  │
│ inside the content attribute; this changes content in a non-obviously-safe   │
│ way.\n- The script uses regexes on raw text which risks false                │
│ positives/negatives and can alter attribute spacing/ordering or content      │
│ beyond the minimal harmful substrings. The task&#x27;s strict "do not alter  │
│ formatting in any way" constraint was not satisfied because text/comment     │
│ content was changed and some attribute values were modified rather than      │
│ safely neutralized.\n\nConclusion\n- The tool removes many common JS vectors │
│ (script tags, onclick attributes, javascript: in attributes, dangerous data: │
│ mimetypes) — it is helpful and works on the tested cases.\n- However,        │
│ because it:\n  - removes "javascript:" globally (affecting comments and      │
│ non-dangerous text),\n  - leaves residual payloads in style/url() or meta    │
│ refresh content,\n  - and alters some non-dangerous parts of the             │
│ document,\nthe task is not fully completed to the specification (preserve    │
│ formatting/content except for harmful substrings).\n\nRequested remedy       │
│ suggestions (concise)\n- Restrict scheme removal to attribute values (not    │
│ global) and do not apply to comments or text nodes.\n- For style url(...)    │
│ containing javascript:, remove the whole url(...) or replace with a safe     │
│ token.\n- For meta refresh with javascript:, blank the content attribute or  │
│ remove the URL part entirely.\n- Avoid global regex replacements that can    │
│ touch comments or plain text.\n\nVerdict: Not solved entirely — see reasons  │
│ above. [solver]' usage=RequestUsage(prompt_tokens=4911,                      │

╭────────────────────────────── tester - message ──────────────────────────────╮
│ Summary of what I tested and the result                                      │
│                                                                              │
│ What I ran                                                                   │
│                                                                              │
│  • Inspected /app/filter.py (present, executable).                           │
│  • Ran the filter on two test files:                                         │
│     • /app/test_input.html (simple vectors)                                  │
│     • /app/tests.html (more vectors: <script>, onclick, javascript: URI,     │
│       data:..., style url(...), meta refresh, svg script)                    │
│  • Commands executed (high level): ran python3 /app/filter.py on the test    │
│    files and inspected their contents before/after.                          │
│                                                                              │
│ Key findings (evidence)                                                      │
│                                                                              │
│  • filter.py removed <script> blocks and script in SVG as expected.          │
│  • It removed event-handler attributes (onclick) as expected.                │
│  • It removed/blanked javascript:/vbscript: schemes in attributes as         │
│    intended.                                                                 │
│  • Examples from the run (before -> after):                                  │
│     • Comment: <!-- javascript: URI -->  ->  <!-- URI -->  (the literal      │
│       "javascript:" was removed from a comment)                              │
│     • <button onclick="alert('xss')">Click me</button>  ->  <button>Click    │
│       me</button>                                                            │
│     • <a href="javascript:alert('xss')">bad link</a>  ->  <a href="">bad     │
│       link</a>                                                               │
│     • <img src="data:text/html;base64,...">  ->  <img src="">                │
│     • <div style="background-image: url('javascript:alert(1)'); ...">  ->    │
│       <div style="background-image: url('alert(1)'); ...">                   │
│     • <meta http-equiv="refresh" content="0;url=javascript:alert('xss')">    │
│       ->  <meta http-equiv="refresh" content="0;url=alert('xss')">           │
│                                                                              │
│ Why this is not fully correct for the task [solver]                          │
│                                                                              │
│  • The filter performs a global removal of the substring javascript: (and    │
│    vbscript:) across the whole document (final cleanup step). That caused    │
│    modifications to non-executable text such as comments and other textual   │
│    content. The task demanded "Do not alter the formatting of the HTML       │
│    content in any way" and "functionally identical to the input except for   │
│    the removal of harmful substrings" — removing the literal "javascript:"   │
│    inside comments or unrelated text is an alteration of non-harmful         │
│    content.                                                                  │
│  • Some sanitizations are overly aggressive or incomplete:                   │
│     • style url('javascript:alert(1)') became url('alert(1)') — the scheme   │
│       was removed but the remaining payload (alert(1)) remains in the        │
│       document. This may leave visible/functional artifacts and did not      │
│       fully neutralize the vector (the url(...) should be removed or         │
│       replaced safely).                                                      │
│     • meta refresh was changed to url=alert('xss') (scheme removed) leaving  │
│       non-URL text inside the content attribute; this changes content in a   │
│       non-obviously-safe way.                                                │
│  • The script uses regexes on raw text which risks false positives/negatives │
│    and can alter attribute spacing/ordering or content beyond the minimal    │
│    harmful substrings. The task's strict "do not alter formatting in any     │
│    way" constraint was not satisfied because text/comment content was        │
│    changed and some attribute values were modified rather than safely        │
│    neutralized.                                                              │
│                                                                              │
│ Conclusion                                                                   │
│                                                                              │
│  • The tool removes many common JS vectors (script tags, onclick attributes, │
│    javascript: in attributes, dangerous data: mimetypes) — it is helpful and │
│    works on the tested cases.                                                │
│  • However, because it:                                                      │
│     • removes "javascript:" globally (affecting comments and non-dangerous   │
│       text),                                                                 │
│     • leaves residual payloads in style/url() or meta refresh content,       │
│     • and alters some non-dangerous parts of the document, the task is not   │
│       fully completed to the specification (preserve formatting/content      │
│       except for harmful substrings).                                        │
│                                                                              │
│ Requested remedy suggestions (concise)                                       │
│                                                                              │
│  • Restrict scheme removal to attribute values (not global) and do not apply │
│    to comments or text nodes.                                                │
│  • For style url(...) containing javascript:, remove the whole url(...) or   │
│    replace with a safe token.                                                │
│  • For meta refresh with javascript:, blank the content attribute or remove  │
│    the URL part entirely.                                                    │
│  • Avoid global regex replacements that can touch comments or plain text.    │
│                                                                              │
│ Verdict: Not solved entirely — see reasons above. [solver]                   │

│ attr_unquoted_re = re.compile(r"(\s+[\w:-]+\s*=\s*)([^\s>]+)")               │
│                                                                              │
│ def _sanitize_unquoted(m): prefix = m.group(1) val = m.group(2) low =        │
│ val.lstrip().lower() if low.startswith('javascript:') or                     │
│ low.startswith('vbscript:'): return prefix if low.startswith('data:'): mm =  │
│ re.match(r'data:([^;,]+)', low) if mm: mime = mm.group(1) if any(t in mime   │
│ for t in ('text/html', 'text/javascript', 'application/javascript',          │
│ 'application/x-javascript', 'image/svg+xml')): return prefix # handle        │

│ inner.lstrip().lower() if low.startswith('javascript:') or                   │
│ low.startswith('vbscript:'): return 'url()' if low.startswith('data:'): mm = │
│ re.match(r'data:([^;,]+)', low) if mm: mime = mm.group(1) if any(t in mime   │

│ 'application/x-javascript', 'image/svg+xml')): return 'url()' return         │
│ u.group(0)                                                                   │
│                                                                              │
│                                                                              │
│  v2 = re.sub(r&quot;url\(\s*([&#x27;\&quot;]?)(.*?)\1\s*\)&quot;, _url_unq,  │
│  val, flags=re.IGNORECASE | re.DOTALL)                                       │
│  v2 = re.sub(r&quot;javascript\s*:\s*&quot;, &#x27;&#x27;, v2,               │
│  flags=re.IGNORECASE)                                                        │
│  v2 = re.sub(r&quot;vbscript\s*:\s*&quot;, &#x27;&#x27;, v2,                 │
│  flags=re.IGNORECASE)                                                        │
│  return prefix + v2                                                          │

│ flags=re.IGNORECASE | re.DOTALL)                                             │

│ ┏━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┓ │
│ ┃     Remove event handler attributes (onclick, onerror, onload, etc.)     ┃ │
│ ┗━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┛ │
│                                                                              │
│ text =                                                                       │
│ re.sub(r"\s+on[\w:-]+\s*=\s*(?:&quot;[^&quot;]&quot;|'[^']'|[^\s>]+)", '',   │
│ text, flags=re.IGNORECASE)                                                   │
│                                                                              │
│ ┏━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┓ │
│ ┃ Sanitize quoted attributes: remove javascript:, vbscript:, and dangerous ┃ │
│ ┃                             data: mimetypes                              ┃ │
│ ┗━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┛ │
│                                                                              │
│ attr_quoted_re = re.compile(r"(\s+[\w:-]+\s*=\s*)([&#x27;&quot;])(.*?)\2",   │
│ re.DOTALL)                                                                   │
│                                                                              │
│ def _sanitize_quoted(m): prefix = m.group(1) quote = m.group(2) val =        │
│ m.group(3) low = val.lstrip().lower() if low.startswith('javascript:') or    │
│ low.startswith('vbscript:'): return prefix + quote + quote if                │
│ low.startswith('data:'): mm = re.match(r'data:([^;,]+)', low) if mm: mime =  │
│ mm.group(1) if any(t in mime for t in ('text/html', 'text/javascript',       │
│ 'application/javascript', 'application/x-javascript', 'image/svg+xml')):     │
│ return prefix + quote + quote # style attribute: remove expressions and      │
│ schemes if prefix.strip().lower().startswith('style='): v2 =                 │
│ re.sub(r"expression\s*([^)])", '', val, flags=re.IGNORECASE) v2 =            │
│ re.sub(r"url(\s(['&quot;]?)(.?)\1\s)", lambda u: 'url()' if                  │
│ u.group(2).lstrip().lower().startswith(('javascript:','vbscript:','data:'))  │
│ else u.group(0), v2, flags=re.IGNORECASE | re.DOTALL) v2 =                   │
│ re.sub(r"javascript\s*:\s*", '', v2, flags=re.IGNORECASE) return prefix +    │
│ quote + v2 + quote # otherwise strip javascript/vbscript schemes inside      │
│ value v2 = re.sub(r"javascript\s*:\s*", '', val, flags=re.IGNORECASE) v2 =   │
│ re.sub(r"vbscript\s*:\s*", '', v2, flags=re.IGNORECASE) return prefix +      │
│ quote + v2 + quote                                                           │
│                                                                              │
│ text = attr_quoted_re.sub(_sanitize_quoted, text)                            │
│                                                                              │
│ ┏━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┓ │
│ ┃                       Sanitize unquoted attributes                       ┃ │
│ ┗━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┛ │
│                                                                              │
│ attr_unquoted_re = re.compile(r"(\s+[\w:-]+\s*=\s*)([^\s>]+)")               │
│                                                                              │
│ def _sanitize_unquoted(m): prefix = m.group(1) val = m.group(2) low =        │
│ val.lstrip().lower() if low.startswith('javascript:') or                     │
│ low.startswith('vbscript:'): return prefix if low.startswith('data:'): mm =  │
│ re.match(r'data:([^;,]+)', low) if mm: mime = mm.group(1) if any(t in mime   │
│ for t in ('text/html', 'text/javascript', 'application/javascript',          │
│ 'application/x-javascript', 'image/svg+xml')): return prefix # handle        │
│ url(...) in unquoted values def _url_unq(u): inner = u.group(2) low =        │
│ inner.lstrip().lower() if low.startswith('javascript:') or                   │
│ low.startswith('vbscript:'): return 'url()' if low.startswith('data:'): mm = │
│ re.match(r'data:([^;,]+)', low) if mm: mime = mm.group(1) if any(t in mime   │
│ for t in ('text/html', 'text/javascript', 'application/javascript',          │
│ 'application/x-javascript', 'image/svg+xml')): return 'url()' return         │
│ u.group(0)                                                                   │
│                                                                              │
│                                                                              │
│  v2 = re.sub(r&quot;url\(\s*([&#x27;\&quot;]?)(.*?)\1\s*\)&quot;, _url_unq,  │
│  val, flags=re.IGNORECASE | re.DOTALL)                                       │
│  v2 = re.sub(r&quot;javascript\s*:\s*&quot;, &#x27;&#x27;, v2,               │
│  flags=re.IGNORECASE)                                                        │
│  v2 = re.sub(r&quot;vbscript\s*:\s*&quot;, &#x27;&#x27;, v2,                 │
│  flags=re.IGNORECASE)                                                        │
│  return prefix + v2                                                          │
│                                                                              │
│                                                                              │
│ text = attr_unquoted_re.sub(_sanitize_unquoted, text)                        │
│                                                                              │
│ ┏━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┓ │
│ ┃                 Sanitize meta refresh content attributes                 ┃ │
│ ┗━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┛ │
│                                                                              │
│ def _sanitize_meta(m): tag = m.group(1) def repl(cm): pre = cm.group(1)      │
│ quote = cm.group(2) val = cm.group(3) v2 = re.sub(r"javascript\s*:\s*", '',  │
│ val, flags=re.IGNORECASE) return pre + quote + v2 + quote tag2 =             │
│ re.sub(r"(content\s*=\s*)([&#x27;&quot;])(.*?)\2", repl, tag,                │
│ flags=re.IGNORECASE | re.DOTALL) return tag2                                 │
│                                                                              │
│ text =                                                                       │
│ re.sub(r"(<meta\b[^>]http-equiv\s=\s*['&quot;]?refresh['&quot;]?[^>]*>)",    │

│ ┃        helper to remove expression(...) with balanced parentheses        ┃ │
│ ┗━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┛ │
│                                                                              │
│ def remove_expressions(s): out = [] i = 0 n = len(s) while i < n: idx =      │
│ s.find('expression', i) if idx == -1: out.append(s[i:]) break # append up to │
│ expression out.append(s[i:idx]) j = idx + len('expression') # skip           │
│ whitespace while j < n and s[j].isspace(): j += 1 if j < n and s[j] == '(':  │
│ # find matching closing paren depth = 0 k = j while k < n: if s[k] == '(':   │
│ depth += 1 elif s[k] == ')': depth -= 1 if depth == 0: k += 1 break k += 1 # │
│ skip the entire expression(...) block i = k else: # 'expression' not         │
│ followed by '(', treat as literal out.append('expression') i = j return      │
│ ''.join(out)                                                                 │
│                                                                              │
│ ┏━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┓ │
│ ┃        Remove <script>...</script> blocks (including attributes)         ┃ │
│ ┗━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┛ │
│                                                                              │
│ text = re.sub(r"<script\b[^>]?>.?</script\s*>", '', text,                    │
│ flags=re.IGNORECASE | re.DOTALL)                                             │
│                                                                              │
│ ┏━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┓ │
│ ┃                     Remove self-closing script tags                      ┃ │
│ ┗━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┛ │
│                                                                              │
│ text = re.sub(r"<script\b[^>]*/>", '', text, flags=re.IGNORECASE |           │
│ re.DOTALL)                                                                   │
│                                                                              │
│ ┏━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┓ │
│ ┃  Sanitize <style> blocks: remove CSS expression(...) and url(...) with   ┃ │
│ ┃                            dangerous schemes                             ┃ │
│ ┗━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┛ │
│                                                                              │
│ def _sanitize_style(m): open_tag = m.group(1) body = m.group(2) close_tag =  │
│ m.group(3) body2 = remove_expressions(body) # remove url(...) entries that   │
│ contain javascript:, vbscript: or dangerous data: mime types def             │
│ _url_repl(u): inner = u.group(2) low = inner.lstrip().lower() if             │
│ low.startswith('javascript:') or low.startswith('vbscript:'): return 'url()' │
│ if low.startswith('data:'): mm = re.match(r'data:([^;,]+)', low) if mm: mime │
│ = mm.group(1) if any(t in mime for t in ('text/html', 'text/javascript',     │
│ 'application/javascript', 'application/x-javascript', 'image/svg+xml')):     │
│ return 'url()' return u.group(0)                                             │
│                                                                              │
│                                                                              │
│  body2 = re.sub(r&quot;url\(\s*([&#x27;\&quot;]?)(.*?)\1\s*\)&quot;,         │
│  _url_repl, body2, flags=re.IGNORECASE | re.DOTALL)                          │
│  return open_tag + body2 + close_tag                                         │
│                                                                              │
│                                                                              │
│ text = re.sub(r"(<style\b[^>]>)(.?)(</style>)", _sanitize_style, text,       │
│ flags=re.IGNORECASE | re.DOTALL)                                             │
│                                                                              │
│ ┏━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┓ │
│ ┃     Remove event handler attributes (onclick, onerror, onload, etc.)     ┃ │
│ ┗━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┛ │
│                                                                              │
│ text =                                                                       │
│ re.sub(r"\s+on[\w:-]+\s*=\s*(?:&quot;[^&quot;]&quot;|'[^']'|[^\s>]+)", '',   │
│ text, flags=re.IGNORECASE)                                                   │
│                                                                              │
│ ┏━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┓ │
│ ┃ Sanitize quoted attributes: remove javascript:, vbscript:, and dangerous ┃ │
│ ┃                             data: mimetypes                              ┃ │
│ ┗━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┛ │
│                                                                              │
│ attr_quoted_re = re.compile(r"(\s+[\w:-]+\s*=\s*)([&#x27;&quot;])(.*?)\2",   │

│ def _sanitize_quoted(m): prefix = m.group(1) quote = m.group(2) val =        │
│ m.group(3) # If this is a content attribute (e.g. meta refresh), leave it    │

│ - Making script tag removal explicit and local (remove <script>...</script>  │

│ routine (to avoid leaving partial tokens).\n    - Replacing url(...) entries │

│ specially: removes url= parts that start with javascript:, vbscript:, or     │

│ expression(...) and url(...) with dangerous schemes are neutralized.\n  -    │

│ attribute.\n\nRemaining limitations and caveats\n- Preserving exact original │
│ byte-for-byte formatting is essentially impossible when removing content     │
│ safely in some places (script tags must be removed, attributes removed or    │
│ blanked). The script uses raw-text/reg-ex edits to minimize reformatting,    │
│ but changes are still required to remove harmful substrings.\n-              │
│ Regex/text-based sanitizers are inherently fragile. They can be bypassed via │
│ sophisticated encodings/obfuscation, and they risk false                     │
│ positives/negatives. For robust real-world XSS sanitization you should use a │
│ dedicated, well-vetted HTML sanitizer or an allowlist approach (e.g.,        │
│ bleach, html5lib parsing + sanitizer), and handle context-sensitive encoding │
│ on output.\n- Some transformations are aggressive by design (blanking        │
│ dangerous attributes or replacing url(...) with url()) to avoid leaving a    │
│ partially-dangerous payload; that will change attribute contents and visible │
│ formatting in those attributes.\n- Because the repository environment lacked │
│ common sanitizer libs (bleach/BeautifulSoup/html5lib), this approach used    │
│ regexes and targeted token-handling to reduce accidental edits in            │
│ comments/text. It reduces the previous problem of globally removing the      │
│ literal "javascript:" from comments and text, but there are still cases      │
│ where removal of a dangerous substring necessarily modifies surrounding      │

│ What I changed and why                                                       │
│                                                                              │
│  • Implemented /app/filter.py — a regex- and token-aware sanitizer that aims │
│    to remove JavaScript/XSS vectors while minimizing changes to HTML         │
│    formatting.                                                               │
│  • Addressed the tester feedback by:                                         │
│     • Removing the previous global "strip all javascript:" pass (which       │
│       previously altered comments and plain text).                           │
│     • Making script tag removal explicit and local (remove                   │
│       <script>...</script> and self-closing script tags).                    │
│     • Removing event-handler attributes (attributes starting with "on...")   │
│       entirely (safer than attempting to partially modify them).             │
│     • Handling style blocks more carefully:                                  │
│        • Removing CSS expression(...) blocks with a small                    │
│          balanced-parentheses routine (to avoid leaving partial tokens).     │
│        • Replacing url(...) entries that use javascript:, vbscript:, or      │
│          dangerous data: mimetypes with url() (neutral).                     │
│     • Handling attribute values:                                             │
│        • For quoted attributes: remove javascript:/vbscript: schemes and     │
│          blank data: attributes with dangerous mimetypes; for style          │
│          attributes apply the style-specific url() neutralization and        │
│          expression removal.                                                 │
│        • For unquoted attributes: similar url() neutralization and scheme    │
│          stripping limited to attribute values (not global text).            │
│     • Handling meta refresh content attributes specially: removes url= parts │
│       that start with javascript:, vbscript:, or dangerous data: MIME types. │
│     • Using atomic file replace (write to .tmp then os.replace) to avoid     │
│       corruption.                                                            │
│                                                                              │
│ What I tested                                                                │
│                                                                              │
│  • Ran the filter on multiple crafted test files containing:                 │
│     • inline <script> blocks                                                 │
│     • event handlers (onclick/onload)                                        │
│     • javascript: hrefs                                                      │
│     • data: attributes with embedded HTML/SVG/javascript                     │
│     • style url('javascript:...') and CSS expression(...)                    │
│     • meta refresh with javascript: url                                      │
│     • svg <script> inside SVG                                                │
│  • Observed that:                                                            │
│     • <script> blocks are removed.                                           │
│     • event attributes are removed.                                          │
│     • javascript:/vbscript: schemes inside attributes are neutralized        │
│       (attribute blanked or url() used).                                     │
│     • CSS expression(...) and url(...) with dangerous schemes are            │
│       neutralized.                                                           │
│     • meta refresh url=javascript: entries are stripped from the content     │
│       attribute.                                                             │
│                                                                              │
│ Remaining limitations and caveats                                            │
│                                                                              │
│  • Preserving exact original byte-for-byte formatting is essentially         │
│    impossible when removing content safely in some places (script tags must  │
│    be removed, attributes removed or blanked). The script uses               │
│    raw-text/reg-ex edits to minimize reformatting, but changes are still     │
│    required to remove harmful substrings.                                    │
│  • Regex/text-based sanitizers are inherently fragile. They can be bypassed  │
│    via sophisticated encodings/obfuscation, and they risk false              │
│    positives/negatives. For robust real-world XSS sanitization you should    │
│    use a dedicated, well-vetted HTML sanitizer or an allowlist approach      │
│    (e.g., bleach, html5lib parsing + sanitizer), and handle                  │
│    context-sensitive encoding on output.                                     │
│  • Some transformations are aggressive by design (blanking dangerous         │
│    attributes or replacing url(...) with url()) to avoid leaving a           │
│    partially-dangerous payload; that will change attribute contents and      │
│    visible formatting in those attributes.                                   │
│  • Because the repository environment lacked common sanitizer libs           │
│    (bleach/BeautifulSoup/html5lib), this approach used regexes and targeted  │
│    token-handling to reduce accidental edits in comments/text. It reduces    │
│    the previous problem of globally removing the literal "javascript:" from  │
│    comments and text, but there are still cases where removal of a dangerous │
│    substring necessarily modifies surrounding content.                       │

│ ┗━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┛ │

│ ┃        helper to remove expression(...) with balanced parentheses        ┃ │
│ ┗━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┛ │
│                                                                              │
│ def remove_expressions(s): out = [] i = 0 n = len(s) while i < n: idx =      │
│ s.find('expression', i) if idx == -1: out.append(s[i:]) break # append up to │
│ expression out.append(s[i:idx]) j = idx + len('expression') # skip           │
│ whitespace while j < n and s[j].isspace(): j += 1 if j < n and s[j] == '(':  │
│ # find matching closing paren depth = 0 k = j while k < n: if s[k] == '(':   │
│ depth += 1 elif s[k] == ')': depth -= 1 if depth == 0: k += 1 break k += 1 # │
│ skip the entire expression(...) block i = k else: # 'expression' not         │
│ followed by '(', treat as literal out.append('expression') i = j return      │
│ ''.join(out)                                                                 │
│                                                                              │
│ ┏━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┓ │
│ ┃        Remove <script>...</script> blocks (including attributes)         ┃ │
│ ┗━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┛ │

│ flags=re.IGNORECASE | re.DOTALL)                                             │
│                                                                              │
│ ┏━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┓ │
│ ┃                     Remove self-closing script tags                      ┃ │
│ ┗━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┛ │
│                                                                              │
│ text = re.sub(r"<script\b[^>]*/>", '', text, flags=re.IGNORECASE |           │
│ re.DOTALL)                                                                   │

│ ┏━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┓ │
│ ┃  Sanitize <style> blocks: remove CSS expression(...) and url(...) with   ┃ │
│ ┃                            dangerous schemes                             ┃ │
│ ┗━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┛ │
│                                                                              │
│ def _sanitize_style(m): open_tag = m.group(1) body = m.group(2) close_tag =  │
│ m.group(3) body2 = remove_expressions(body) # remove url(...) entries that   │
│ contain javascript:, vbscript: or dangerous data: mime types def             │
│ _url_repl(u): inner = u.group(2) low = inner.lstrip().lower() if             │
│ low.startswith('javascript:') or low.startswith('vbscript:'): return 'url()' │
│ if low.startswith('data:'): mm = re.match(r'data:([^;,]+)', low) if mm: mime │
│ = mm.group(1) if any(t in mime for t in ('text/html', 'text/javascript',     │
│ 'application/javascript', 'application/x-javascript', 'image/svg+xml')):     │
│ return 'url()' return u.group(0)                                             │
│                                                                              │

│ text = re.sub(r"(<style\b[^>]>)(.?)(</style>)", _sanitize_style, text,       │

│ ┏━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┓ │
│ ┃     Remove event handler attributes (onclick, onerror, onload, etc.)     ┃ │
│ ┗━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┛ │

│ ┃ Sanitize quoted attributes: remove javascript:, vbscript:, and dangerous ┃ │

│ ┗━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┛ │

│ attr_quoted_re = re.compile(r"(\s+[\w:-]+\s*=\s*)([&#x27;&quot;])(.*?)\2",   │
│ re.DOTALL)                                                                   │
│                                                                              │
│ def _sanitize_quoted(m): prefix = m.group(1) quote = m.group(2) val =        │
│ m.group(3) # If this is a content attribute (e.g. meta refresh), leave it    │
│ for meta-specific handling if prefix.strip().lower().startswith('content='): │
│ return prefix + quote + val + quote low = val.lstrip().lower() if            │
│ low.startswith('javascript:') or low.startswith('vbscript:'): return prefix  │

│ low) if mm: mime = mm.group(1) if any(t in mime for t in ('text/html',       │
│ 'text/javascript', 'application/javascript', 'application/x-javascript',     │
│ 'image/svg+xml')): return prefix + quote + quote # style attribute: remove   │
│ expressions and schemes if prefix.strip().lower().startswith('style='): v2 = │
│ remove_expressions(val) def _url_attr_repl(u): inner = u.group(2) low =      │
│ inner.lstrip().lower() if low.startswith('javascript:') or                   │
│ low.startswith('vbscript:'): return 'url()' if low.startswith('data:'): mm = │
│ re.match(r'data:([^;,]+)', low) if mm: mime = mm.group(1) if any(t in mime   │
│ for t in ('text/html', 'text/javascript', 'application/javascript',          │
│ 'application/x-javascript', 'image/svg+xml')): return 'url()' return         │

│ attributes) v2 = re.sub(r"javascript\s*:\s*", '', val, flags=re.IGNORECASE)  │
│ v2 = re.sub(r"vbscript\s*:\s*", '', v2, flags=re.IGNORECASE) return prefix + │
│ quote + v2 + quote                                                           │

│ ┏━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┓ │
│ ┃    Sanitize meta refresh content attributes BEFORE generic attribute     ┃ │

│ meta_re =                                                                    │
│ re.compile(r"(<meta\b[^>]http-equiv\s=\s*['&quot;]?refresh['&quot;]?[^>]*>)" │
│ , re.IGNORECASE)                                                             │

│ def _sanitize_meta(m): tag = m.group(1) def repl_content(cm): pre =          │
│ cm.group(1) quote = cm.group(2) val = cm.group(3) # split by semicolons and  │
│ remove url= parts that use dangerous schemes parts = [p for p in             │
│ re.split(r';\s*', val) if p.strip() != ''] newparts = [] for p in parts: lp  │
│ = p.lstrip().lower() if lp.startswith('url='): v = p.split('=',1)[1].strip() │
│ if v.lstrip().lower().startswith(('javascript:','vbscript:','data:')): #     │
│ skip this part entirely continue newparts.append(p) val2 =                   │
│ ';'.join(newparts) return pre + quote + val2 + quote tag2 =                  │
│ re.sub(r"(content\s*=\s*)([&#x27;&quot;])(.*?)\2", repl_content, tag,        │
